### Internal functions
- `_fft_square(a)`: use power-of-two N = next pow2(2n-1), rfft, irreft, clip small negative to 0? For gradient weights, negative values from roundoff may make max slightly under; use `np.maximum(b, 0)` and perhaps rescale sum? For soft max, negative small irrelevant. For score, max_b maybe 0 for zeros. Use `float(np.max(b))`.
- `_fft_grad_a(p, a)`: Need compute 2∑_j a_j p_{i+j}. We can do using reversed a convolution with p as derived. Let L=2n-1. Need h_i = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j p_{i+j}, i=0..n-1. We can compute with `np.fft.irfft(F_rev_a * F_p, N)[:?]?` Let's derive offset carefully to implement without errors.
Let ra[j]=a[n-1-j] length n. Let p length L. Convolution c = ra * p (linear). c[r]=∑_{j=0}^{n-1} ra[j] p[r-j] = ∑ a[n-1-j] p[r-j]. Let i = r - (n-1). Then c[n-1+i]=∑ a[n-1-j] p[i+n-1-j]. Set j' = n-1-j => j = n-1-j'. Sum j'=0..n-1: a[j'] p[i + j']. Yes. So h_i = c[n-1+i]. Great. So compute conv length n+L-1 =3n-2. Use FFT length pow2(3n-2) maybe. `fa_rev = rfft(ra, N)`, `fp = rfft(p, N)`, `c=irfft(fa_rev*fp,N)`, return `2*c[n-1:n-1+n]`. Good. Check n=2: a=[a0,a1], ra=[a1,a0]. h0=c1? c=ra*p: c1 = ra0 p1 + ra1 p0 = a1 p1 + a0 p0. expected h0=a0p0+a1p1. OK. h1=c2=ra0 p2+ra1p1=a1p2+a0p1. expected a0p1+a1p2. Good.
- `_make_weights(b, tau, eps)`: p = softmax(b / tau) maybe with shift max. To avoid underflow: z=(b-m)/tau. `s=exp(z).sum()`. If tau very small, underflow; set p one-hot over argmax if s tiny. Add epsilon uniform. But if b length large, tau in units max_b (~0.5/n ~1e-4); need scale. We can use relative: z=(b-m)/ (tau_max * max_b) where tau_max dimensionless. Let `temp = soft_temp * max_b`. For focus previous: z=(maxb-b)/maxb, p=exp(-focus*z) = softmax((b - maxb)/maxb * focus) equivalent τ=maxb/focus. So focus=1/τ_rel. Use focus range [1,10000]. p concentrated near max. For Adam, p probabilities; no need eps unless tau tiny; use stabilization: if focus > 50 maybe p over indices with b > maxb - delta? Could use top-k? Softmax underflow ok with shift; if all exp underflow to 0, set top max one-hot. Add eps=1e-6 maybe.

### Optimizers

#### Fast eval & scoring
```python
def _next_pow2(x): ...
def _fft_square(a):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,n=N)[:L].real
    # tiny negative due to roundoff; for max we can set negative to 0? But if actual max small negative impossible. Use np.maximum(b,0)
    b = np.maximum(b,0.0)
    return b
```
But clipping negative to 0 can undercount if true b negative? b nonnegative (convolution of nonnegative), so ok. Rounding can make central slightly below true by relative 1e-12; safe margin. Maybe don't clip all? `b[b<0]=0` only.
Score: `2*n*b.max()/(a.sum()**2)`. Since we keep sum1, `2*n*bmax`. For general normalize.

#### Gradient h
```python
def _fft_grad_a(p,a):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(L+n-1) # 3n-2
    ra=a[::-1].copy()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(ra,n=N)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,n=N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fp,n=N)
    return 2.0 * c[n-1:n-1+n]
```
We should check length: a length n, p length L, c length n+L-1=3n-2. Indices c[n-1:n-1+n] length n. For N power2 at least 3n-2; use `_next_pow2(n+L-1)`.
Potential overflow? p sum1, a sum1, values ~1/n, gradient ~1/n? ok.

#### Weights
```python
def _weights_from_b(b, focus, eps=0.0):
    maxb=b.max()
    if maxb <= 0: return uniform
    if focus >= 10000: p = onehot at argmax (maybe tie all max)
    else:
       z=(b - maxb) * focus / maxb
       exp_z=np.exp(np.clip(z, -700, 0))
       s=exp_z.sum()
       if s < 1e-300: p onehot max
       else: p=exp_z/s
       if eps>0: p=(1-eps)*p + eps/L
    return p
```
Since z<=0. If focus large, underflow for all but max; set onehot ok. But if many exactly max due flat, exp all 1; onehot only if s tiny. Good.

Objective for line search with p: expected φ = dot(p,b). We can use for gradient. For hard score, use b.max().

#### Multiplicative optimizer (simplex)
Pseudo:
```
def _opt_multiplicative(a0, deadline, rng, focus0=5, label=''):
    a = normalize positive
    v? none
    focus=focus0; eta=0.1; eta_min=1e-4; eta_max=5; no_improve=0; best=inf; best_a=a.copy()
    while now<deadline:
       b=_fft_square(a); score=...
       update best
       if no_improve>... reset
       p=_weights(... focus)
       g=_fft_grad_a(p,a)
       gd=g - np.dot(a,g)  # log gradient (actually for φ)
       scale=np.max(np.abs(gd)); if tiny break
       gd/=scale
       # propose candidates with several steps
       best_local_score=score; best_local=a
       for alpha in [eta*4,eta,eta/4, eta/16]? choose best hard score
           cand = a*np.exp(-alpha*gd)
           cand=np.maximum(cand,1e-300); cand/=cand.sum()
           b_new/ score_new; if score_new < best_local choose
       if improved score or best: accept eta*=1.1 else eta*=0.8
       focus=min(focus*inc,max)
```

- Return normalized list.

### Optimizer details

#### `_internal_score(a)`
```
def _fix_a(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
   a=np.nan_to_num(a)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-12 or a.size<2: ...
   return a/s
```
Score: if a.size n, b=_fft_square(a), maxb, score=2*n*maxb/(s*s). Since normalized sum=1.

#### Full multiplicative optimizer `_opt_flow(a0, deadline, rng, focus_start, label)`
We'll implement with actual time deadline; each loop iterate until deadline. Use `while time.monotonic() < deadline`.

- a normalized, positive >=1e-12.
- focus = focus_start
- eta = 0.05? Previous normalized gradient; alpha range maybe 0.02-1. Let's start 0.1.
- `best_score`, `best_a`.
- no_improve, resets.
- optionally `momentum`: accumulate direction `m_dir = beta*m_dir + (1-beta)*gd` and use for step. Could help. Let's do momentum beta=0.9 for full? Mirror descent with momentum can accelerate but nonconvex. Use small beta 0.8.
   gd_norm = gd/scale. m = beta*m + (1-beta)*gd_norm. candidate uses m. If m all zero, break.
- Acceptance:
   For candidate, compute b_new, score_new. Also weighted phi_old, phi_new = dot(p,b_new). Accept if score_new < score - eps or score_new < score and phi_new < phi_old - eps? Use tolerance. If accepted, update a,b,score, eta*=1.03. Else eta*=0.8; if eta<eta_min, perturb a (mix best/fresh or noise) and reset b.
- Focus increases: if accepted maybe focus*=1.005, if rejected focus*=1.05? To avoid stuck, cycle focus via random choices on resets.
- Patience: if no_improve > max_no (e.g. 800) do reset:
   resets++
   if resets>20 break.
   Create new a:
      base = best_a
      fresh = random anti/log based on n or full random. For full, fresh = `_random_smooth_full(n,rng,amp)` maybe log-normal with anti? Could use projection?    focus random low.
   no_improve=0.

#### Anti line search optimizer `_opt_anti(x0, deadline, rng, ...)`
Input x length m (n=2m). We can also handle odd n? Maybe skip odd. Build a.
Loop:
- Compute b/score if dirty.
- update best_x.
- p=weights b focus.
- ga=_fft_grad_a(p,a)
- D = ga[:m] - ga[m:][::-1]
- scale=max(abs(D)); if tiny break.
- gd = D/scale; momentum `m = beta*m +(1-beta)*gd`.
- Step alphas: If line_search, try `[eta*2, eta, eta*0.5]`; else `[eta]`.
   For each alpha: x_c=clip(x - alpha*m, eps,1-eps). Build a_c, b_c score_c. Also phi=dot(p,b_c).
   Choose candidate with highest improvement in tuple (score, phi). Accept if score_c < score - tol or (score_c < score + tol and phi<phi_old - tol) or score_c<best.
- Accept: update x,a,b, improve counters, eta*=1.05 maybe; focus slow.
- Reject: eta*=0.8; if eta<min, perturb x: mix best_x with fresh (random truncated normal/anti smooth/rounded) and reset.
- If no_improve > patience resets.

Because x boundaries: if m direction all clipped, maybe break.

#### Let's design specific functions now.

```python

`_normalize_a(a)`:
- ensure finite, clip [0,1000], if sum<=0.01 fallback uniform 1024.
- return a / sum.

`_random_full(n, rng, amp=2.0, anti=False, smooth=False)`:
```
if anti and n even:
   m=n//2
   v=np.zeros(m)
   for f in (1,2,3,5,7): v += (rng.normal(0,1)/f)*np.sin(2π f i/m + rng.uniform(...))
   v += 0.2 rng.normal
   v = np.tanh(amp*v)
   x=0.5*(1+v)
   return _a_from_x(x)
else:
   v=np.zeros(n)
   if smooth: add sin terms
   else: rng.normal
   v=v-max; a=exp(v); normalize
```
But for full optimizer, random uniform with anti low-freq.

`_a_from_x(x)`:
```
m=len(x); n=2*m; t=1.0/m; a=np.empty(n); a[:m]=t*x; a[m:]=t*(1-x[::-1]); return a
```
Note if x in [0,1], sum=1. Good.

`_x_from_a(a)`: For even,pairs i and n-1-i may not sum constant. Project: c_i = a_i + a_comp; if c_i>0, x_i=a_i/c_i. But pair sums c_i may not all equal; need normalized anti. We can set `c = a[:m]+a[m:][::-1]`, total C=sum c. Want pair sum uniform 2/n. We can scale c: x_i = (a_i / c_i)/(C/(n/2))? More simply target pair mass p=2/n. Let y_i = a_i / (target?) We want y_i + y_comp = p. We can define half-mass `u_i = (a_i - a_comp)/2 + p/2 = a_i +?` If we set x_i=(u_i)/(p), with u_i in [0,p]. u_i = (a_i + target? formula). Let p=2/n. Want x_i = u_i/p, where u_i = (a_i - a_comp)/2 + p/2 = a_i + (p - a_i - a_comp)/2? Since a_i+a_comp=c_i. Choose x_i=(a_i - a_comp)/(c_i - p) + 1/2? But if c_i not p, ambiguous. We can first redistribute pair mass uniformly while preserving difference: u_i = (a_i - a_comp)/(c_i?)? Better: z_i = a_i - a_comp, and desired u_i = (z_i)/2 + p/2. This preserves difference but changes sum to p regardless: u_i + u_comp = p. Then x_i = u_i/p. Clip [eps,1-eps]. This may change total mass but sum becomes 1. Good. Use for anti projection.

#### `_opt_full`
Let's code carefully with top-level functions.
```
def _opt_full(a0, deadline, rng, focus_start=5.0, focus_end=5000.0, label=''):
    a=_normalize_a(a0)
    n=a.size
    b=None; score=inf
    best_score=inf; best_a=a.copy()
    focus=float(focus_start)
    eta=0.1
    eta_min=1e-4; eta_max=10.0
    beta=0.8
    mom=np.zeros(n)
    no=0; max_no=600; resets=0; max_resets=20
    # estimated time per iter? not.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if b is None:
           b=_fft_square(a); score= _quick_score(a,b)
       if score < best_score - 1e-12: update best, no=0
       else: no+=1; if no>max_no: reset ...
       p=_weights(b,focus)
       g=_fft_grad(p,a)
       gd=g - np.dot(a,g)
       scale=np.max(np.abs(gd))
       if scale <=1e-18: break
       gd=gd/scale
       mom = beta*mom + (1.0-beta)*gd
       # if too many zeros? 
       if np.max(np.abs(mom))<1e-12: perturb
       # candidate generation: one or three alphas
       cands = []
       alphas = [eta]
       if no>20 or resets>0: alphas=[eta*2, eta, eta*0.5]
       best_candidate=None; best_score_c=score; best_phi_c=dot(p,b)
       for alpha in alphas:
           cand=a*np.exp(-alpha*mom)
           cand=np.maximum(cand, 1e-300)
           s=cand.sum()
           if s<=0: continue
           cand/=s
           b_c=_fft_square(cand)
           sc=_quick_score(cand,b_c)
           ph=dot(p,b_c)
           if sc < best_score_c - 1e-12 or (abs(sc-best_score_c)<=1e-12 and ph<best_phi_c-1e-12): choose
       if best_candidate is not None and (best_score_c < score - 1e-12 or best_score_c < best_score - 1e-12 or (best_phi_c < dot(p,b)-1e-10 and best_score_c < score*1.001)):
           accept ...
       else reject ...
       focus *=1.01 if accepted else 1.03; clip
       it+=1
```
This is a bit complex. We can simplify: Always evaluate one candidate with alpha=eta; if no improvement after 5, try line search 3? But code okay.
Need be careful: `best_phi_c` should be phi of current? `phi_old = np.dot(p,b)`. Candidate chosen if phi lower and score not too high.
Accept criteria: If candidate score improves current or best, or score within 0.2% and phi improved by >1e-10. Use `tol=1e-12`.
Reset: Use fresh generator based on n. For even n, mix best and anti; for odd full random.
```
def _make_fresh(n,rng,base=None, anti_weight=0.5):
   if n%2==0 and anti_weight>0:
       m=n//2; x=_anti_x_smooth(m,rng,amp=3.0)
       cand=_a_from_x(x)
       if base is not None: cand=(1-anti_weight)*base + anti_weight*cand; normalize
   else: full random smooth
```
If base None, random.

We need not print too much; can log.

#### `_sign_refine_api`
Implement with binary x and in-place b copy. Need careful O(n) update.
For binary x (0/1): m,n. Pair index i: l=i, r=n-1-i. If x_i=1, a_l=t, a_r=0. If x_i=0, a_l=0,a_r=t, where t=1/m=2/n. Flip: delta_l = -2*t*x_i? If x=1, new first 0 delta_l=-t? Wait old first=t, new first=0: delta_l=-t. But t=1/m, not 2/n? Since n=2m, 1/m=2/n. Earlier I used 2/n; yes delta=-2/n? 1/m=2/n. old second=0, new second=t: delta_r=+t. For x=0: delta_l=+t, delta_r=-t. Let d = t*(1 - 2*x_i) for l, d_r=t*(2*x_i-1)=-d. Since t=1/m.
Quadratic terms values d².
Update b_trial = b.copy(); for pos l value dl=d:
   b_trial[l:l+n] += 2*dl*a
   b_trial[2*l] += dl*dl
 for pos r value dr=-d:
   b_trial[r:r+n] += 2*dr*a
   b_trial[2*r] += dr*dr
 b_trial[l+r] += 2*dl*dr (since l+r < 2n-1; l+r = n-1 for all? Because r=n-1-l => l+r=n-1, central! Quadratic cross always at center. For binary flip, dl*dr=-d², so central decreases by 2d² if d² positive? interesting.)
Then maxb_new=np.max(b_trial). If accept, b=b_trial, modify a[l]+=dl; a[r]+=dr; score update. If reject, discard.
Potential negative due roundoff? b_trial nonnegative but due subtract may negative at some entries; np.max unaffected if true max positive. Could clip? For score, negative small okay? max maybe if all negative impossible. We can use `np.maximum(b_trial,0).max()` each accept? For rejection score, if negative at affected, true b nonnegative; max can ignore negatives unless all near? Use `maxb=np.max(b_trial); if maxb<0` improbable. But for consistency `maxb=float(np.max(np.maximum(b_trial,0.0)))`.
To reduce copy cost, we can update in place and revert if rejected: 
```
old = b.copy() # same cost
```
Copy unavoidable. But we can update b in place and keep delta values to revert if reject (revert O(n) anyway). Copy simpler.

Proposal: random index. To bias to recent active, we can maintain count of active lags contribution. For a random active lag k (top p sample), we need choose i such that l=i contributes if a_{k-l}>0? Could be heavy. Random okay.

Because score = 2n maxb, score differences maybe ~1e-6. T=0.001 allows big accepted early.

```

#### Use **submodular greedy direct optimization for b max**
We can construct a sequence directly to minimize max b by adding mass to indices with smallest marginal increase. Start with small mass all zero; allocate total mass one by one to index that minimizes max convolution after adding. Since objective scale invariant and quadratic, water-filling. For large continuous, we can approximate with discrete small increments. Greedy: maintain a, b counts/coeffs. At each step, for each index i, delta if add small δ to a_i increases b_k by 2δ a_{k-i}+δ². Choose i with smallest maximum increase; iterate. This is like submodular? This is similar to previous greedy support for binary but for continuous weights. Not.

 Let's draft code mentally and ensure no closures/lambdas.

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _pow2(x):
    x=int(x)
    if x<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()
```

`_clean_sequence`:
```
def _clean_sequence(seq):
    s=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    s=np.nan_to_num(s, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    s=np.clip(s,0.0,1000.0)
    t=s.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(t) or t < 0.01:
        s=np.ones(1024)/1024.0
    else:
        s=s/t
    return s
```
`_safe_exact(seq)`:
```
def _safe_exact(seq):
    try:
       ev=evaluate_sequence
    except NameError:
       return inf
    if not callable(ev): return inf
    try:
       val=ev([float(x) for x in seq])
       if np.isfinite(val): return float(val)
    except Exception: pass
    return inf
```
But `evaluate_sequence` will be in globals; accessing `evaluate_sequence` directly allowed? The rule says don't import, assume imported. If name not defined during local testing, NameError. Use globals.

`_fft_square`, `_fft_grad_a`, `_score_from_b` as above.

`_weights`:
```
def _weights(b, focus):
    b=np.asarray(b)
    L=b.size
    maxb=float(b.max())
    if maxb <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L,1.0/L)
    if focus >= 250.0:
        topk=int(max(32, min(L, int(np.sqrt(L)*2))))
        # If focus higher, smaller topk
        topk=int(max(16, min(L, topk // max(1, int((focus-250)/500)))))
        idx=np.argpartition(b, -topk)[-topk:]
        p=np.zeros(L)
        p[idx]=1.0/topk
        return p
    scale=maxb
    z=(b-maxb)*(float(focus)/scale)
    z=np.clip(z,-700.0,0.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p=np.zeros(L); p[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0; return p
    p=e/s
    eps= min(0.02, 1.0/float(focus)) if focus>1 else 0.0
    if eps>0:
       p=(1-eps)*p+eps/L
    return p
```

Implement `_anti_x_smooth`:
```
def _anti_x_smooth(m, rng, amp=2.0, freqs=(1,2,3,5,7), sign=False):
    x=np.arange(m)
    v=np.zeros(m)
    for f in freqs:
       amp_f = float(rng.normal(0.0, 2.0)) / float(f)
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       v += amp_f*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x/m + phase)
    v += 0.25*rng.normal(0,1,m)
    if sign:
       v = np.sign(v)
       v[v==0]=1.0
    else:
       v=np.tanh(amp*v / (np.std(v)+1e-12))
    v=np.clip(v,-1,1)
    return 0.5*(1.0+v)
```
Maybe ensure not all same by normalizing? If std small, set random.

`_anti_x_greedy`:
```
def _anti_x_greedy(n, rng, order='random'):
   m=n//2
   counts=np.zeros(2*n-1,np.int64)
   selected=np.empty(m,np.int64)
   t=0
   if order=='center':
      idx=np.arange(m-1,-1,-1) # center-out? pair l=m-1,r=m first
   elif order='left': idx=np.arange(m)
   else: idx=np.arange(m); rng.shuffle(idx)
   for idx_val in idx:
      l=int(idx_val); r=n-1-l
      if t==0:
         y = l if rng.random()<0.5 else r? For deterministic maybe l.
      else:
         s=selected[:t]
         # vectorized max counts for affected
         # `counts[s + l]` advanced array length t (could be large); okay
         # To save memory/time, maybe only sample if t large? t up to 4096, fine.
         aff_l=np.max(counts[s+l]) if t>0 else 0
         self_l=counts[2*l]
         metric_l=max(int(aff_l), int(self_l)) + (2 if t>0 else 1)?
```
Previous metric: `affected_l + 2, self_l + 1` if t>0. Why 2? Because when adding y, for each old point, count at y+old increases by 2 (ordered); self increases by1. The immediate max after addition at affected indices is old_count +2, self old+1. If multiple same, max. It uses max(affected_l+2, self_l+1). Let's replicate.
```

self_l=int(counts[2*l])
metric_l=max(affected_l+2, self_l+1)
...
y=l if metric_l <= metric_r else r
```
Then update:
```

counts[2*y]+=1
selected[t]=y; t+=1
```
At t=0, update counts[2y]+=1.
After, x=np.full(m,0.0); x[selected < m]=1.0 else 0. Note if we choose r (>=m), first side 0.

`_random_full`: For full fresh:
```
def _random_full(n, rng, anti=False, smooth=True, amp=2.0):
    if anti and n%2==0:
       m=n//2; x=_anti_x_smooth(m,rng,amp=amp, freqs=(1,2,3,5,7,11), sign=False)
       return _a_from_x(x)
    v=np.zeros(n)
    if smooth:
       for f in (1,2,3,5,7,11,13):
          v += (float(f)/max(1,n))*rng.normal(0,1)*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*np.arange(n)/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    v += rng.normal(0,1,n)
    v = v - np.max(v)
    a=np.exp(v*amp)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-15); a/=a.sum(); return a
```

def _opt_full(a0, deadline, rng, focus_start=10.0, label=''):
    a=_normalize_positive(a0)
    n=a.size
    b=None; score=inf
    best_score=inf; best_a=a.copy()
    focus=float(focus_start)
    eta=0.08
    eta_min=1e-4; eta_max=5.0
    beta=0.85
    mom=np.zeros(n)
    no=0; max_no=500; resets=0; max_resets=15; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if b is None:
          b=_fft_square(a); score=_quick_score(a,b)
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no=0
       else:
          no += 1
       if no > max_no:
          resets += 1
          if resets > max_resets: break
          # reset
          a=_make_full_refresh(n, best_a, rng, resets)
          b=None; score=inf
          mom=np.zeros(n)
          focus=float(rng.choice([3.0,5.0,10.0,20.0,50.0,100.0]))
          eta=0.08
          no=0; max_no=int(max_no*1.2)
          continue
       p=_weights(b,focus)
       g=_fft_grad_a(p,a)
       gd=g - float(np.dot(a,g))
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(gd)))
       if scale < 1e-18: break
       gd/=scale
       mom = beta*mom + (1.0-beta)*gd
       mscale=float(np.max(np.abs(mom)))
       if mscale < 1e-15: perturb
       mom/=mscale
       # candidate number based on time? use 1 normally, 3 every 10?
       alphas=[eta]
       if no > 50 or (it % 20 == 0): alphas=[eta*2.0, eta, eta*0.5]
       best_cand=None; best_sc=score; best_phi=float(np.dot(p,b))
       phi_old=best_phi
       for alpha in alphas:
          cand=a*np.exp(-alpha*mom)
          cand=np.maximum(cand,1e-300)
          ss=float(cand.sum())
          if ss <= 1e-300: continue
          cand/=ss
          b_c=_fft_square(cand)
          sc=_quick_score(cand,b_c)
          ph=float(np.dot(p,b_c))
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-12 or (abs(sc-best_sc) <= 1e-12 and ph < best_phi - 1e-12):
              best_cand=cand; best_sc=sc; best_phi=ph
       accept=False
       if best_cand is not None:
          if best_sc < score - 1e-12: accept=True
          elif best_sc < best_score - 1e-12: accept=True
          elif best_sc < score*1.001 and best_phi < phi_old - 1e-10: accept=True
       if accept:
          a=best_cand; b=_fft_square(a)? Wait we already have b_c for chosen, but we didn't store. We can store b_c in best_cand_b.
```

Reset function `_make_full_refresh(n, base, rng, reset_count)`:
```
def _make_full_refresh(n, base, rng, reset_count):
    if n%2==0 and (reset_count%2==0 or rng.random()<0.5):
       m=n//2
       # choose init type random: smooth, greedy, random binary
       r=rng.random()
       if r<0.4:
           x=_anti_x_smooth(m,rng,amp=3.0)
       elif r<0.6:
           x=_anti_x_greedy(n,rng,order='center')
       elif r<0.8:
           x=_anti_x_greedy(n,rng,order='random')
       else:
           x=_anti_x_smooth(m,rng,amp=5.0,sign=True)
       cand=_a_from_x(x)
    else:
       cand=_random_full(n,rng,anti=False,smooth=True,amp=3.0)
    if base is not None:
       mix = 0.3 + 0.4*rng.random()
       cand=(1-mix)*base + mix*cand
    return _normalize_positive(cand)
```
This resets to fresh.

Potential issue: full optimizer with momentum and normalized gradient may oscillate. Acceptance may be too strict. We can allow accept if phi improved a lot even score slightly worse, but over many iters may drift. Use previous.

def _make_anti_x_refresh(n, best_x, rng, reset_count):
   m=n//2
   r=rng.random()
   if r<0.4: x=_anti_x_smooth(m,rng,amp=3.0)
   elif r<0.65: x=_anti_x_greedy(n,rng, order = ['center','left','random'][reset_count%3])
   elif r<0.85: x=np.clip(0.5 + 0.4*rng.normal(0,1,m), eps,1-eps) # random
   else: x=_anti_x_smooth(m,rng,amp=6.0,sign=True)
   if best_x is not None:
      mix=0.3+0.4*rng.random()
      x=(1-mix)*best_x + mix*x

#### `_sign_refine` code in detail
```
def _sign_refine(x0, deadline, rng, focus=None):
    m=int(x0.size); n=2*m
    # binary x: round
    x=(np.where(x0>0.5, 1.0, 0.0)).astype(np.float64)
    t=1.0/m
    a=_a_from_x(x,n)
    b=_fft_square(a)
    maxb=float(b.max()); score=2*n*maxb
    best_score=score; best_x=x.copy(); best_maxb=maxb
    T=0.002
    no=0; max_no=2000; it=0
    # maybe initial p? not needed
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if no > max_no:
           # reheat or reset to best + random flips
           if rng.random()<0.5: T=max(T*5,0.0005)
           else:
              x=best_x.copy()
              # random flip 5% 
              flip=np.where(rng.random(m)<0.05,1.0,0.0)
              x=np.where(flip,1-x,x)
              a=_a_from_x(x,n); b=_fft_square(a); maxb=...; score=...
           no=0; max_no=int(max_no*1.2)
       i=int(rng.integers(0,m))
       d=t*(1.0 - 2.0*x[i])
       if abs(d)<1e-12: continue
       l=i; r=n-1-i
       b2=b.copy()
       # update linear
       b2[l:l+n] += 2*d*a
       b2[r:r+n] += -2*d*a
       # quadratic
       b2[2*l] += d*d
       b2[2*r] += d*d
       b2[l+r] += -2*d*d   # since dr=-d, 2*d*dr=-2d²
       # ensure no negative? not necessary
       new_max=float(b2.max())
       if new_max < 0: new_max=0.0
       new_score=2*n*new_max
       if new_score < score - 1e-12:
           accept=True
       else:
           if T > 1e-8:
               # compute exponent safe
               delta=(new_score-score)/(T+1e-300)
               if delta < 50 and rng.random() < exp(-delta): accept=True
               else accept=False
           else accept=False
       if accept:
           b=b2
           a[l]+=d; a[r]-=d
           x[i]=1.0 - x[i]
           maxb=new_max; score=new_score
           no=0
           if score < best_score - 1e-12:
               best_score=score; best_x=x.copy(); best_maxb=maxb
       else:
           no +=1
       # cool
       T = min(T*0.9999, 0.01)?? Wait T*0.9999 after 100k = 0.002*0.135=0.00027 ok.
       T *= 0.9999
       if it % 500 == 0:
          # recompute b to avoid drift (cost)
          b=_fft_square(a); maxb=max; if maxb < 0:0; score=2nmaxb; no=0
       it +=1
    return best_x, best_score
```
Check quadratic update: For flip binary, old a_l = t*x, old a_r=t*(1-x). Let d change at l = t*(1-2x). dr=-d. New a_l=old+d, a_r=old+dr.
Change b = 2d a shifted l + 2dr a shifted r + d^2 at 2l + dr^2 at 2r + 2d dr at l+r. dr=-d, cross=-2d². Good.
But linear update uses old a or new? For (a+δ)*(a+δ), cross terms use old a. We used a old before updating. Good.
Does updating b2[r:r+n] with -2*d*a overlap with l slice if they overlap? Yes addition handles.
Central cross l+r=n-1 always. For l+r = n-1; within b length yes.
If 2l or 2r equals l+r? If l=r? n even, l<r; no. 2l may equal l+r if l=r no. Different indices.
Potential negative at central due subtract; fine.
Time: b2.copy + two slice adds + max. For n=5582, b length11163, fine.

Could use `np.dot`? No.

After sign refine, best_x binary. We'll refine with anti optimizer.

Function `_anti_project` returns a (not x):
```
def _anti_project(a):
    n=a.size
    if n%2==0:
       m=n//2; t=1.0/m
       left=a[:m]; right=a[m:][::-1]
       z = (left - right) * 0.5 + t*0.5
       x = z / t
       x = np.clip(x, 1e-9, 1-1e-9)
       return _a_from_x(x,n)
    return None
```
Check: desired first = t x, second = t(1-x). Difference first-second = t(2x-1)= (left-right)? We set z=t x = (left-right)/2 + t/2 yes. Pair sum t. Good.

### Let's formulate final tasks precisely.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380.0):
    start=time.monotonic()
    if budget_s is None: total=2380
    else: total=float(budget_s)
    if not np.isfinite(total) or total<120: total=120
    total=min(total, 2380.0)
    deadline = start + total - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_a=None; best_score=inf; given_a=None; given_exact=inf
    # read given
    given = globals().get('height_sequence_1')
    if given is not None:
       ga=np.asarray(given,np.float64).ravel()
       ga=clean
       s=ga.sum()
       if s>0.01 and ga.size>20:
          ga=ga/s
          sc,b=_fft_square(ga) ...
          best_a=ga; best_score=sc
          given_a=ga.copy()
          # exact if len <= 12000 and time
          exact=_safe_exact(list(ga)) if len<=12000 else inf
          given_exact=exact
```

### Let's test code logic for gradient sign
Full optimizer descent: `gd = g - dot(a,g)`. If we move `a * exp(-alpha * gd)`, does that decrease φ? For softmax log gradient is exactly `a_i(dφ_i - ∑a_j dφ_j)`. Wait `a_i` factor means the direction in log space is `dφ_i - mean`. The multiplicative update on a uses exponent of `dφ_i - mean` (without multiplying by a? previous used gd = grad - dot(a,grad), then a*exp(-eta gd). Let's derive: log a_new = log a - eta*(dφ_i - mean). Then a_new = a_i exp(-eta(dφ_i-mean)). Good. No extra a_i. So previous correct. For anti x, x not log; we use D = g_first-g_second; direction x - alpha*D_norm. This is projected gradient, not mirror. Could use logit mirror for x: r=log(x/(1-x)), direction r? But simpler.

Maybe for anti, using mirror on pair probability (x) is better: x_new= x*(1-x)*exp(-eta D)? Since x∈(0,1), the natural metric for Bernoulli is logit; gradient in logit is D/(x(1-x)). But previous linear x update okay. We could implement logit with `r=np.log(x/(1-x))`, update r - eta* D_norm? Then x=sigmoid(r). This prevents saturation? Actually gradient in r is D/(x(1-x)); normalizing loses. Simpler: use x linear but add periodic resample boundary.

Could we optimize pair x with **exponentiated weights** like x_new = x*(1-x)/(sum?)? No single.

Maybe convert anti x to a full and run full optimizer on a but with projection to anti after each update: `a_new_full = multiplicative step`, then `a_proj = anti_project(a_new_full)`, accept if score improves. This may use robust full update but constrained. We can implement a task `_opt_anti_proj`? But we already have anti. Could be better. But code length.

### Maybe use **Simultaneous multiple p weights (robust max)**
Instead of one p from softmax, use a convex combination of several foci: e.g. p = 0.5*weights(focus) + 0.5*weights(focus*10). This blends broad and sharp. We can implement in `_weights` by averaging two temps. But not necessary.

Could use **random p sampling**: each iteration choose p with focus random from range, creating stochastic subgradient. Previous resets. We can add in full optimizer: focus drift with Ornstein-Uhlenbeck: `focus = clip(focus*1.02 + rng.normal(0,0.2))`. But focus should generally increase.

### Ensure no syntax errors, all functions top-level. No closures or lambda. Use `float` lists.

Potential code:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _pow2(x):
    x=int(x)
    if x<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()

def _clean_seq(seq):
    arr=np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01 or arr.size < 2:
        arr=np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)/1024.0
    else:
        arr = arr / s
    return arr

def _fft_square(a):
    n=int(a.size)
    L=2*n-1
    N=_pow2(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=N)[:L].real
    # clip tiny negatives
    b[b < 0.0] = 0.0
    return b

def _score_with_b(a,b):
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return float('inf'), float(b.max())
    maxb=float(b.max())
    return float(2.0*a.size*maxb/(s*s)), maxb

def _fft_grad_a(p,a):
    n=int(a.size)
    L=2*n-1
    N=_pow2(n + L - 1)
    ra=a[::-1].copy()
    fr=np.fft.rfft(ra, n=N)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p, n=N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fr*fp, n=N)
    out=c[n-1:n-1+n]
    return 2.0*out
```
Check N: n+L-1=3n-2; `_pow2(3n-2)` OK. For n=1? not.

`_weights`:
```
def _weights(b, focus):
    L=int(b.size)
    maxb=float(b.max())
    if maxb <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0/L, dtype=np.float64)
    focus=float(focus)
    if focus >= 500.0:
        topk=int(max(32, min(L, int(4.0*np.sqrt(L)))))
        idx=np.argpartition(b, -topk)[-topk:]
        p=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[idx]=1.0/topk
        return p
    z=(b - maxb) * (focus / maxb)
    z=np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        return p
    p=e/s
    if focus > 1.0:
        eps=min(0.05, 2.0/focus)
        p=(1.0-eps)*p + (eps/L)
    return p
```
If focus=0? not.

`_a_from_x`:
```
def _a_from_x(x, n=None):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m=int(x.size)
    if n is None: n=2*m
    t=1.0/m
    a=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m]=t*x
    a[m:]=t*(1.0 - x[::-1])
    return a
```

`_x_from_anti_a(a)`:
```
def _x_from_anti_a(a):
    n=int(a.size); m=n//2; t=1.0/m
    x=a[:m]/t
    x=np.clip(x,1e-8,1.0-1e-8)
    return x
```

`_anti_project_a(a)`:
```
def _anti_project_a(a):
    n=int(a.size)
    if n%2: return None
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m=n//2; t=1.0/m
    left=a[:m]; right=a[m:][::-1]
    z=(left - right)*0.5 + 0.5*t
    x=z/t
    x=np.clip(x,1e-8,1.0-1e-8)
    return _a_from_x(x,n)
```

`_anti_x_smooth`:
```
def _anti_x_smooth(m, rng, amp=2.0, freqs=(1,2,3,5,7,11), sign=False):
    m=int(m)
    if m<=0: return np.array([0.5])
    idx=np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    v=np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    for f in freqs:
       coef=float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) / float(f)
       phase=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
       v += coef * np.sin(2.0*np.pi*float(f)*idx/m + phase)
    v += 0.25*float(amp)*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,size=m)
    std=float(np.std(v))
    if std < 1e-12:
       v = rng.normal(0.0,1.0,size=m)
       std=1.0
    v = v / std
    if sign:
       v = np.where(v >= 0.0, 1.0, -1.0)
    else:
       v = np.tanh(amp * v)
    return 0.5*(1.0 + np.clip(v,-1.0,1.0))
```

`_anti_x_greedy` as above.
Need order parameter string.
```
def _anti_x_greedy(n, rng, order='random'):
    n=int(n); m=n//2
    counts=np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.int64)
    selected=np.empty(m, dtype=np.int64)
    if order == 'center':
        order_arr=np.arange(m-1, -1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
    elif order == 'left':
        order_arr=np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        order_arr=np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
        rng.shuffle(order_arr)
    t=0
    for val in order_arr:
        l=int(val); r=n-1-l
        if t == 0:
            y = l if rng.random() < 0.5 else r
        else:
            s=selected[:t]
            al=int(np.max(counts[s+l]))
            self_l=int(counts[2*l])
            metric_l=max(al+2, self_l+1)
            ar=int(np.max(counts[s+r]))
            self_r=int(counts[2*r])
            metric_r=max(ar+2, self_r+1)
            y=l if metric_l <= metric_r else r
        if t > 0:
            counts[selected[:t]+y] += 2
        counts[2*y] += 1
        selected[t]=y
        t+=1
    x=np.full(m, 0.0, dtype=np.float64)
    x[selected < m] = 1.0
    return x
```
Could `counts[s+l]` where s+l are numpy int arrays, fine. If t large, okay.

`_random_full`:
```
def _random_full(n, rng, anti=False, smooth=True, amp=2.0):
    n=int(n)
    if anti and n%2==0:
        m=n//2
        r=float(rng.random())
        if r < 0.5:
           x=_anti_x_smooth(m,rng,amp=amp)
        elif r < 0.75:
           x=_anti_x_greedy(n,rng,order='center')
        elif r < 0.9:
           x=_anti_x_greedy(n,rng,order='random')
        else:
           x=_anti_x_smooth(m,rng,amp=amp*2.0,sign=True)
        return _a_from_x(x,n)
    idx=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    v=np.zeros(n)
    if smooth:
       for f in (1,2,3,5,7,11):
          coef=float(rng.normal(0.0,1.0))/float(f)
          phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
          v += coef*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*idx/n + phase)
    v += float(amp)*rng.normal(0,1,size=n)
    v = v - float(np.max(v))
    a=np.exp(v)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-15)
    return a/float(a.sum())
```

`_safe_exact`:
```
def _safe_exact(seq):
    ev=globals().get('evaluate_sequence')
    if ev is None or not callable(ev): return float('inf')
    try:
        val=ev([float(x) for x in seq])
        val=float(val)
        if np.isfinite(val): return val
    except Exception:
        pass
    return float('inf')
```

`_make_full_refresh`:
```
def _make_full_refresh(n, base, rng, reset_count):
    n=int(n)
    r=float(rng.random())
    if n%2==0 and (r<0.65 or reset_count%2==0):
       m=n//2
       rr=float(rng.random())
       if rr<0.35:
          x=_anti_x_smooth(m,rng,amp=3.0)
       elif rr<0.55:
          x=_anti_x_greedy(n,rng,order='center')
       elif rr<0.75:
          x=_anti_x_greedy(n,rng,order='random')
       elif rr<0.9:
          x=_anti_x_smooth(m,rng,amp=6.0,sign=True)
       else:
          x=np.clip(0.5 + 0.4*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,size=m), 1e-6, 1.0-1e-6)
       cand=_a_from_x(x,n)
    else:
       cand=_random_full(n,rng,anti=False,smooth=True,amp=3.0)
    if base is not None:
       mix=0.25 + 0.5*float(rng.random())
       cand=(1.0-mix)*base + mix*cand
    a=_clean_seq(cand)
    return a
```

`_make_anti_x_refresh`:
```
def _make_anti_x_refresh(n, best_x, rng, reset_count):
    n=int(n); m=n//2
    rr=float(rng.random())
    if rr<0.35:
       x=_anti_x_smooth(m,rng,amp=3.0)
    elif rr<0.55:
       x=_anti_x_greedy(n,rng,order='center')
    elif rr<0.75:
       x=_anti_x_greedy(n,rng,order='left')
    elif rr<0.9:
       x=_anti_x_smooth(m,rng,amp=6.0,sign=True)
    else:
       x=np.clip(0.5 + 0.5*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,size=m), 1e-6,1.0-1e-6)
    if best_x is not None:
       mix=0.25+0.5*float(rng.random())
       x=(1.0-mix)*best_x + mix*x
    return np.clip(x,1e-6,1.0-1e-6)
```

`_opt_full` detailed. Need be mindful of infinite loops if best_tuple remains None. Let's implement concise.
```
def _opt_full(a0, deadline, rng, focus_start=10.0, label=''):
    a=_clean_seq(a0)
    n=int(a.size)
    b=None; score=inf
    best_score=inf; best_a=a.copy()
    focus=float(focus_start)
    eta=0.08
    eta_min=1e-4; eta_max=8.0
    beta=0.85
    mom=np.zeros(n)
    no=0; max_no=600; resets=0; max_resets=20; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline):
       if b is None:
           b=_fft_square(a)
           score,_=_score_with_b(a,b)
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no=0
       else:
           no += 1
       if no > max_no:
           resets += 1
           if resets > max_resets: break
           a=_make_full_refresh(n, best_a, rng, resets)
           b=None; score=inf
           mom=np.zeros(n)
           focus=float(rng.choice(np.array([3.0,5.0,10.0,20.0,50.0,100.0,200.0])))
           eta=0.08
           no=0; max_no=int(max_no*1.2 if max_no<3000 else max_no)
           continue
       p=_weights(b, focus)
       g=_fft_grad_a(p,a)
       gd=g - float(np.dot(a,g))
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(gd)))
       if scale < 1e-18:
           # stochastic perturbation
           a=_make_full_refresh(n, best_a, rng, resets)
           b=None; mom=np.zeros(n); no=0; focus=max(3.0,focus*0.5); continue
       gd = gd / scale
       mom = beta*mom + (1.0-beta)*gd
       mscale=float(np.max(np.abs(mom)))
       if mscale < 1e-15:
           mom = gd
           mscale=1.0
       mom = mom / mscale
       alphas=(eta,)
       if no > 50 or (it % 25 == 0):
           alphas=(eta*2.0, eta, eta*0.5)
       best_tuple=None; best_sc=score; best_phi=float(np.dot(p,b)); phi_old=best_phi
       for alpha in alphas:
           cand=a*np.exp(-alpha*mom)
           cand=np.maximum(cand, 1e-300)
           ss=float(cand.sum())
           if ss <= 1e-300: continue
           cand = cand/ss
           b_c=_fft_square(cand)
           sc,_=_score_with_b(cand,b_c)
           ph=float(np.dot(p,b_c))
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-12 or (abs(sc-best_sc) <= 1e-12 and ph < best_phi - 1e-12):
               best_tuple=(cand,b_c); best_sc=sc; best_phi=ph
       accept=False
       if best_tuple is not None:
           if best_sc < score - 1e-12:
               accept=True
           elif best_sc < best_score - 1e-12:
               accept=True
           elif best_sc < score*1.001 and best_phi < phi_old - 1e-10:
               accept=True
       if accept:
           a,b=best_tuple
           score=best_sc
           eta=min(eta*1.06, eta_max)
           focus=min(focus*1.015, 2000.0)
       else:
           eta=max(eta*0.8, eta_min)
           focus=min(focus*1.04, 2000.0)
           if eta <= eta_min*1.2:
              # nudge
              a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n))
              a=_clean_seq(a)
              b=None; no=0
       it += 1
    if score < best_score:
        best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
    return best_a, float(best_score)
```
Potential issue: `np.exp(-alpha*mom)` where mom normalized, alpha up to 8, okay. `phi_old` can be dot(p,b) for current b; after b None? we ensure b. Good.
If best_tuple None? we initialize best_sc=score; no accept. Good.
Could `score*1.001` overflow? no.

def _opt_anti(x0, deadline, rng, focus_start=10.0, label=''):
    m=int(x0.size); n=2*m
    x=np.clip(np.asarray(x0,dtype=np.float64).ravel(),1e-6,1.0-1e-6)
    a=_a_from_x(x,n)
    b=None; score=inf
    best_score=inf; best_x=x.copy()
    focus=float(focus_start); eta=0.08; eta_min=1e-4; eta_max=8.0; beta=0.85
    mom=np.zeros(m); no=0; max_no=600; resets=0; max_resets=20; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if b is None:
          b=_fft_square(a); score,_=_score_with_b(a,b)
       if score < best_score - 1e-12: update best_x
       else: no+=1
       if no > max_no: reset x/_a, continue
       p=_weights(b,focus)
       g=_fft_grad_a(p,a)
       D=g[:m] - g[m:][::-1]
       scale=max(np.abs(D)); if tiny reset/perturb
       D/=scale
       mom=beta*mom + (1.0-beta)*D; normalize
       alphas as full
       for alpha: xc=clip(x-alpha*mom, eps,1-eps); if not any changed? perturb
         ac=_a_from_x(xc,n); bc; sc; ph; choose
       accept similar
       if accept update x,a,b
       else eta reduce, focus inc, if eta small perturb x with rng normal
       it++
```

def _next_paley_prime(start, max_delta=2000):

    if p%4!=3: p += (3 - p%4)%4? 
    while ...:
       if p %4 ==3 and _is_prime(p): return p
       p += 4

def _paley_seed(p):
    w=np.full(p,-1.0)
    if p>2:
      w[0]=1.0
      for i in range(1,p):
         if pow(i,(p-1)//2,p)==p-1: w[i]=1.0
    a=(1.0+w)/float(p)
    return a

def _opt_anti(x0, deadline, rng, focus_start=10.0, label=''):
    m=int(x0.size); n=2*m
    x=np.clip(np.asarray(x0, np.float64).ravel(), 1e-6, 1.0-1e-6)
    a=_a_from_x(x,n)
    b=None; score=inf
    best_score=inf; best_x=x.copy()
    focus=float(focus_start)
    eta=0.08; eta_min=1e-4; eta_max=8.0
    beta=0.85
    mom=np.zeros(m)
    no=0; max_no=600; resets=0; max_resets=20; it=0
    eps=1e-6
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if b is None:
          b=_fft_square(a); score,_=_score_with_b(a,b)
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=score; best_x=x.copy(); no=0
       else: no +=1
       if no > max_no:
          resets +=1
          if resets>max_resets: break
          x=_make_anti_x_refresh(n,best_x,rng,resets)
          a=_a_from_x(x,n)
          b=None; score=inf; mom=np.zeros(m); focus=float(rng.choice(...)); eta=0.08
          no=0; max_no=min(3000,int(max_no*1.2))
          continue
       p=_weights(b,focus)
       g=_fft_grad_a(p,a)
       D=g[:m]-g[m:][::-1]
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(D)))
       if scale<1e-18:
          x=_make_anti_x_refresh(...); ... continue
       D/=scale
       mom=beta*mom+(1-beta)*D
       mscale=... normalize
       alphas=(eta,)
       if no>50 or it%25==0: alphas=(eta*2,eta,eta*0.5)
       best_tuple=None; best_sc=score; best_phi=dot(p,b); phi_old=best_phi
       for alpha in alphas:
          xc=x-alpha*mom
          xc=np.clip(xc,eps,1-eps)
          if not np.any(xc != x): continue
          ac=_a_from_x(xc,n)
          bc=_fft_square(ac)
          sc,_=_score_with_b(ac,bc)
          ph=dot(p,bc)
          if better choose
       accept...
       if accept: x,a,b from tuple; score; eta*... focus*1.015
       else: eta*... focus*1.04; if eta small: x=np.clip(x+rng.normal(0,0.05,m),eps,1-eps); a=...; b=None; no=0
       it +=1
    if b is None or score<best... return a from best_x? Need return a.
    best_a = _a_from_x(best_x,n); best_s,_= _score... maybe best_score.
    if b is not None and score<best_score: best_x=x.copy(); best_score=score; best_a=_a_from_x(best_x,n)
    return best_a, float(best_score)

### Let's now produce final answer.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

The search combines:
  - warm-started multiplicative mirror-descent on the full simplex,
  - anti-palindromic low-dimensional mirror/projected descent,
  - discrete sign-flip local search for anti-palindromic supports,
  - structured initializations (smooth anti, greedy anti, Paley character seeds),
  - small-length probes, diversity restarts, and final exact-score validation.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _clean_seq(seq):
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01 or arr.size < 2:
        arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    else:
        arr = arr / s
    return arr


def _fft_square(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N)[:L].real
    b[b < 0.0] = 0.0
    return b


def _score_with_b(a, b):
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf"), float(b.max())
    maxb = float(b.max())
    return float(2.0 * a.size * maxb / (s * s)), maxb


def _fft_grad_a(p, a):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k p[k] * (a*a)[k] with respect to a.

    Returns d/d a_i = 2 * sum_j a[j] p[i+j], where p is zero outside [0,2n-2].
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _pow2(n + L - 1)
    ra = a[::-1].copy()
    fr = np.fft.rfft(ra, n=N)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, n=N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fr * fp, n=N)
    out = c[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    return 2.0 * out


def _weights(b, focus):
    L = int(b.size)
    maxb = float(b.max())
    if maxb <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)

    focus = float(focus)

    # Very high focus: uniform on a top block.
    if focus >= 500.0:
        if L <= 64:
            return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        topk = int(max(32, min(L, int(4.0 * np.sqrt(L)))))
        if topk >= L:
            return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -topk)[-topk:]
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[idx] = 1.0 / float(topk)
        return p

    # Smooth max-approximation weights.
    z = (b - maxb) * (focus / maxb)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        return p

    p = e / s
    if focus > 1.0:
        eps = min(0.05, 2.0 / focus)
        p = (1.0 - eps) * p + (eps / float(L))
    return p


def _a_from_x(x, n=None):
    """
    Anti-palindromic ansatz for even length n=2m:
       a_i = t*x_i,
       a_{n-1-i} = t*(1-x_i),
       t = 1/m.
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(x.size)
    if n is None:
        n = 2 * m
    t = 1.0 / float(m)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m] = t * x
    a[m:] = t * (1.0 - x[::-1])
    return a


def _x_from_anti_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = n // 2
    t = 1.0 / float(m)
    x = a[:m] / t
    return np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)


def _anti_project_a(a):
    """Project an even-length vector to the anti-palindromic pair-sum subspace."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return None
    m = n // 2
    t = 1.0 / float(m)
    left = a[:m]
    right = a[m:][::-1]
    z = 0.5 * (left - right) + 0.5 * t
    x = z / t
    x = np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
    return _a_from_x(x, n)


def _anti_x_smooth(m, rng, amp=2.0, freqs=(1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 11), sign=False):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([0.5], dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    for f in freqs:
        coef = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) / float(f)
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        v += coef * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * idx / float(m) + phase)
    v += 0.25 * float(amp) * float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) * np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    std = float(np.std(v))
    if std < 1e-12:
        v = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=m)
        std = 1.0
    v = v / std
    if sign:
        v = np.where(v >= 0.0, 1.0, -1.0)
    else:
        v = np.tanh(amp * v)
    v = np.clip(v, -1.0, 1.0)
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + v)


def _anti_x_greedy(n, rng, order="random"):
    """
    Greedy construction of one element from each anti-pair.
    Tries to keep pair-sum collision counts low.
    """
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    counts = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int64)
    selected = np.empty(m, dtype=np.int64)

    if order == "center":
        order_arr = np.arange(m - 1, -1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
    elif order == "left":
        order_arr = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        order_arr = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
        rng.shuffle(order_arr)

    t = 0
    for val in order_arr:
        l = int(val)
        r = n - 1 - l

        if t == 0:
            y = l if rng.random() < 0.5 else r
        else:
            s = selected[:t]
            aff_l = int(np.max(counts[s + l]))
            self_l = int(counts[2 * l])
            metric_l = max(aff_l + 2, self_l + 1)

            aff_r = int(np.max(counts[s + r]))
            self_r = int(counts[2 * r])
            metric_r = max(aff_r + 2, self_r + 1)

            y = l if metric_l <= metric_r else r

        if t > 0:
            counts[selected[:t] + y] += 2
        counts[2 * y] += 1
        selected[t] = y
        t += 1

    x = np.full(m, 0.0, dtype=np.float64)
    x[selected < m] = 1.0
    return x


def _random_full(n, rng, anti=False, smooth=True, amp=2.0):
    n = int(n)
    if anti and (n % 2 == 0):
        m = n // 2
        r = float(rng.random())
        if r < 0.50:
            x = _anti_x_smooth(m, rng, amp=amp)
        elif r < 0.70:
            x = _anti_x_greedy(n, rng, order="center")
        elif r < 0.88:
            x = _anti_x_greedy(n, rng, order="random")
        else:
            x = _anti_x_smooth(m, rng, amp=amp * 2.0, sign=True)
        return _a_from_x(x, n)

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if smooth:
        for f in (1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 11):
            coef = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) / float(f)
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            v += coef * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * idx / float(n) + phase)
    v += float(amp) * float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = v - float(np.max(v))
    a = np.exp(v)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
    return a / float(a.sum())


def _make_full_refresh(n, base, rng, reset_count):
    n = int(n)
    r = float(rng.random())
    if (n % 2 == 0) and (r < 0.65 or (reset_count % 2 == 0)):
        m = n // 2
        rr = float(rng.random())
        if rr < 0.35:
            x = _anti_x_smooth(m, rng, amp=3.0)
        elif rr < 0.55:
            x = _anti_x_greedy(n, rng, order="center")
        elif rr < 0.75:
            x = _anti_x_greedy(n, rng, order="random")
        elif rr < 0.90:
            x = _anti_x_smooth(m, rng, amp=6.0, sign=True)
        else:
            x = np.clip(0.5 + 0.4 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=m), 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)
        cand = _a_from_x(x, n)
    else:
        cand = _random_full(n, rng, anti=False, smooth=True, amp=3.0)

    if base is not None:
        base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        mix = 0.25 + 0.50 * float(rng.random())
        cand = (1.0 - mix) * base + mix * cand

    return _clean_seq(cand)


def _make_anti_x_refresh(n, best_x, rng, reset_count):
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    rr = float(rng.random())
    if rr < 0.35:
        x = _anti_x_smooth(m, rng, amp=3.0)
    elif rr < 0.55:
        x = _anti_x_greedy(n, rng, order="center")
    elif rr < 0.75:
        x = _anti_x_greedy(n, rng, order="left")
    elif rr < 0.90:
        x = _anti_x_smooth(m, rng, amp=6.0, sign=True)
    else:
        x = np.clip(0.5 + 0.5 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=m), 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)

    if best_x is not None:
        best_x = np.asarray(best_x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        mix = 0.25 + 0.50 * float(rng.random())
        x = (1.0 - mix) * best_x + mix * x

    return np.clip(x, 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)


def _paley_best_seed(p=8191):
    """
    Construct two Paley-style character seeds of length p (p should be 3 mod 4 prime):
      support = quadratic residues + 0, or non-residues + 0.
    Return the seed with the better internal FFT score.
    """
    p = int(p)
    if p <= 2:
        return np.ones(p, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, p))

    is_res = np.zeros(p, dtype=bool)
    is_res[0] = True
    e = (p - 1) // 2
    for i in range(1, p):
        if pow(i, e, p) == 1:
            is_res[i] = True

    is_non = ~is_res
    is_non[0] = True

    w_res = np.where(is_res, 1.0, -1.0)
    w_non = np.where(is_non, 1.0, -1.0)

    a_res = (1.0 + w_res) / float(p)
    a_non = (1.0 + w_non) / float(p)

    a_res = _clean_seq(a_res)
    a_non = _clean_seq(a_non)

    br = _fft_square(a_res)
    bn = _fft_square(a_non)
    sr, _ = _score_with_b(a_res, br)
    sn, _ = _score_with_b(a_non, bn)

    if sn < sr:
        return a_non, float(sn)
    return a_res, float(sr)


def _opt_full(a0, deadline, rng, focus_start=10.0, label=""):
    a = _clean_seq(a0)
    n = int(a.size)

    b = None
    score = float("inf")

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_a = a.copy()

    focus = float(focus_start)
    eta = 0.08
    eta_min = 1e-4
    eta_max = 8.0
    beta = 0.85
    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    no_improve = 0
    max_no = 600
    resets = 0
    max_resets = 20
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline):
        if b is None:
            b = _fft_square(a)
            score, _ = _score_with_b(a, b)

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > max_no:
            resets += 1
            if resets > max_resets:
                break
            a = _make_full_refresh(n, best_a, rng, resets)
            b = None
            score = float("inf")
            mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            focus = float(rng.choice(np.array([3.0, 5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 50.0, 100.0, 200.0])))
            eta = 0.08
            no_improve = 0
            max_no = int(max_no * 1.2) if max_no < 3000 else max_no
            it = 0
            continue

        p = _weights(b, focus)
        g = _fft_grad_a(p, a)
        gd = g - float(np.dot(a, g))
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(gd)))

        if scale < 1e-18:
            a = _make_full_refresh(n, best_a, rng, resets)
            b = None
            score = float("inf")
            mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            focus = max(3.0, focus * 0.5)
            no_improve = 0
            it = 0
            continue

        gd = gd / scale
        mom = beta * mom + (1.0 - beta) * gd
        mscale = float(np.max(np.abs(mom)))
        if mscale < 1e-15:
            mom = gd
            mscale = 1.0
        mom = mom / mscale

        if no_improve > 50 or (it % 25 == 0):
            alphas = (eta * 2.0, eta, eta * 0.5)
        else:
            alphas = (eta,)

        phi_old = float(np.dot(p, b))
        best_tuple = None
        best_sc = score
        best_phi = phi_old

        for alpha in alphas:
            cand = a * np.exp(-alpha * mom)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-300)
            ss = float(cand.sum())
            if ss <= 1e-300:
                continue
            cand = cand / ss
            b_c = _fft_square(cand)
            sc, _ = _score_with_b(cand, b_c)
            ph = float(np.dot(p, b_c))
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-12 or (abs(sc - best_sc) <= 1e-12 and ph < best_phi - 1e-12):
                best_tuple = (cand, b_c)
                best_sc = sc
                best_phi = ph

        accept = False
        if best_tuple is not None:
            if best_sc < score - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif best_sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif best_sc < score * 1.001 and best_phi < phi_old - 1e-10:
                accept = True

        if accept:
            a, b = best_tuple
            score = best_sc
            eta = min(eta * 1.06, eta_max)
            focus = min(focus * 1.015, 2000.0)
        else:
            eta = max(eta * 0.8, eta_min)
            focus = min(focus * 1.04, 2000.0)
            if eta <= eta_min * 1.2:
                a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=n))
                a = _clean_seq(a)
                b = None
                no_improve = 0

        it += 1

    if b is not None and score < best_score:
        best_score = score
        best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def _opt_anti(x0, deadline, rng, focus_start=10.0, label=""):
    m = int(x0.size)
    n = 2 * m
    eps = 1e-6

    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), eps, 1.0 - eps)
    a = _a_from_x(x, n)

    b = None
    score = float("inf")

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_x = x.copy()

    focus = float(focus_start)
    eta = 0.08
    eta_min = 1e-4
    eta_max = 8.0
    beta = 0.85
    mom = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    no_improve = 0
    max_no = 600
    resets = 0
    max_resets = 20
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline):
        if b is None:
            b = _fft_square(a)
            score, _ = _score_with_b(a, b)

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best_x = x.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > max_no:
            resets += 1
            if resets > max_resets:
                break
            x = _make_anti_x_refresh(n, best_x, rng, resets)
            a = _a_from_x(x, n)
            b = None
            score = float("inf")
            mom = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            focus = float(rng.choice(np.array([3.0, 5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 50.0, 100.0, 200.0])))
            eta = 0.08
            no_improve = 0
            max_no = int(max_no * 1.2) if max_no < 3000 else max_no
            it = 0
            continue

        p = _weights(b, focus)
        g = _fft_grad_a(p, a)
        D = g[:m] - g[m:][::-1]
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(D)))

        if scale < 1e-18:
            x = _make_anti_x_refresh(n, best_x, rng, resets)
            a = _a_from_x(x, n)
            b = None
            score = float("inf")
            mom = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            focus = max(3.0, focus * 0.5)
            no_improve = 0
            it = 0
            continue

        D = D / scale
        mom = beta * mom + (1.0 - beta) * D
        mscale = float(np.max(np.abs(mom)))
        if mscale < 1e-15:
            mom = D
            mscale = 1.0
        mom = mom / mscale

        if no_improve > 50 or (it % 25 == 0):
            alphas = (eta * 2.0, eta, eta * 0.5)
        else:
            alphas = (eta,)

        phi_old = float(np.dot(p, b))
        best_tuple = None
        best_sc = score
        best_phi = phi_old

        for alpha in alphas:
            xc = x - alpha * mom
            xc = np.clip(xc, eps, 1.0 - eps)
            if not np.any(xc != x):
                continue

            ac = _a_from_x(xc, n)
            bc = _fft_square(ac)
            sc, _ = _score_with_b(ac, bc)
            ph = float(np.dot(p, bc))

            if sc < best_sc - 1e-12 or (abs(sc - best_sc) <= 1e-12 and ph < best_phi - 1e-12):
                best_tuple = (xc, ac, bc)
                best_sc = sc
                best_phi = ph

        accept = False
        if best_tuple is not None:
            if best_sc < score - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif best_sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif best_sc < score * 1.001 and best_phi < phi_old - 1e-10:
                accept = True

        if accept:
            x, a, b = best_tuple
            score = best_sc
            eta = min(eta * 1.06, eta_max)
            focus = min(focus * 1.015, 2000.0)
        else:
            eta = max(eta * 0.8, eta_min)
            focus = min(focus * 1.04, 2000.0)
            if eta <= eta_min * 1.2:
                x = np.clip(x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=m), eps, 1.0 - eps)
                a = _a_from_x(x, n)
                b = None
                no_improve = 0

        it += 1

    if b is not None and score < best_score:
        best_score = score
        best_x = x.copy()

    best_a = _a_from_x(best_x, n)
    return best_a, float(best_score)


def _sign_refine(x0, deadline, rng):
    """
    Local search over binary anti-palindromic choices.
    Uses exact O(n) convolution updates for single flips.
    """
    m = int(x0.size)
    n = 2 * m
    t = 1.0 / float(m)

    x = np.where(np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel() > 0.5, 1.0, 0.0).astype(np.float64)
    a = _a_from_x(x, n)
    b = _fft_square(a)
    maxb = float(b.max())
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * maxb

    best_score = score
    best_x = x.copy()

    T = 0.0020
    no_improve = 0
    max_no = 2500
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline):
        if no_improve > max_no:
            if rng.random() < 0.45:
                T = max(T * 5.0, 0.0005)
            else:
                x = best_x.copy()
                flip = (rng.random(m) < 0.06)
                x = np.where(flip, 1.0 - x, x)
                a = _a_from_x(x, n)
                b = _fft_square(a)
                maxb = float(b.max())
                score = 2.0 * float(n) * maxb
            no_improve = 0
            max_no = int(max_no * 1.15) if max_no < 40000 else max_no
            continue

        i = int(rng.integers(0, m))
        d = t * (1.0 - 2.0 * x[i])
        if abs(d) < 1e-14:
            continue

        l = i
        r = n - 1 - i

        b2 = b.copy()

        # Linear terms: 2 * delta * a, where delta has two nonzero entries.
        b2[l:l + n] += 2.0 * d * a
        b2[r:r + n] -= 2.0 * d * a

        # Quadratic terms.
        b2[2 * l] += d * d
        b2[2 * r] += d * d
        b2[l + r] -= 2.0 * d * d

        new_max = float(b2.max())
        if new_max < 0.0:
            new_max = 0.0
        new_score = 2.0 * float(n) * new_max

        accept = False
        if new_score < score - 1e-12:
            accept = True
        else:
            if T > 1e-8:
                delta = (new_score - score) / (T + 1e-300)
                if delta < 50.0 and rng.random() < np.exp(-delta):
                    accept = True

        if accept:
            b = b2
            a[l] += d
            a[r] -= d
            x[i] = 1.0 - x[i]
            maxb = new_max
            score = new_score
            no_improve = 0

            if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = score
                best_x = x.copy()
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        T = min(T * 0.9999, 0.01)
        if T < 1e-8:
            T = 1e-8

        # Periodically recompute b to remove accumulated floating drift.
        if (it % 700 == 0):
            b = _fft_square(a)
            maxb = float(b.max())
            if maxb < 0.0:
                maxb = 0.0
            score = 2.0 * float(n) * maxb
            no_improve = 0

        it += 1

    best_a = _a_from_x(best_x, n)
    return best_a, float(best_score)


def _threshold_anti_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return None
    m = n // 2
    x = (a[:m] >= a[m:][::-1]).astype(np.float64)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return _a_from_x(x, n)


def _safe_exact(seq):
    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence")
    if ev is None or not callable(ev):
        return float("inf")
    try:
        val = ev([float(x) for x in seq])
        val = float(val)
        if np.isfinite(val):
            return val
    except Exception:
        pass
    return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380.0):
    """
    Main search entry point. Returns a list of non-negative floats.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()

    if budget_s is None:
        total = 2380.0
    else:
        total = float(budget_s)
    if not np.isfinite(total) or total <= 30.0:
        total = 60.0
    total = min(total, 2380.0)
    end = start + total - 25.0
    budget = max(1.0, end - start)

    seed = int(seed)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    print(f"optimizer start: budget={total:.1f}s")

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    given_a = None
    given_exact = float("inf")

    # Warm start from previous best if available.
    given_raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    if given_raw is not None:
        try:
            g = _clean_seq(given_raw)
            if g.size > 10:
                gb = _fft_square(g)
                gsc, _ = _score_with_b(g, gb)
                if np.isfinite(gsc):
                    best_a = g.copy()
                    best_score = gsc
                    given_a = g.copy()
                    print(f"given internal score={gsc:.9f}, n={g.size}")
                    if g.size <= 12000 and (end - time.monotonic()) > 8.0:
                        given_exact = _safe_exact(g)
                        print(f"given exact score={given_exact:.9f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = _random_full(4096, rng, anti=True, smooth=True, amp=2.0)
        b = _fft_square(best_a)
        best_score, _ = _score_with_b(best_a, b)
        print(f"fallback random initial score={best_score:.9f}, n={best_a.size}")

    def _consider(cand, sc, name):
        nonlocal best_a, best_score
        try:
            cand = _clean_seq(cand)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                bb = _fft_square(cand)
                sc, _ = _score_with_b(cand, bb)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                best_a = cand
                best_score = float(sc)
                print(f"update best via {name}: {best_score:.9f}, n={best_a.size}")
                return True
        except Exception:
            pass
        return False

    # Phase 0: small-n probes. Cheap, and occasionally reveal odd-length basins.
    for small_n in (7, 9, 11, 13, 17):
        now = time.monotonic()
        limit = min(budget * 0.012, max(0.0, end - 20.0 - now))
        if limit < 8.0:
            break
        init = _random_full(small_n, rng, anti=(small_n % 2 == 0), smooth=True, amp=2.0)
        cand, sc = _opt_full(init, now + limit, rng, focus_start=5.0)
        _consider(cand, sc, f"small_n={small_n}")

    # Phase 1: polish the provided best sequence, if meaningful.
    if given_a is not None:
        now = time.monotonic()
        limit = min(budget * 0.20, max(0.0, end - 45.0 - now))
        if limit > 20.0:
            print(f"phase given full, limit={limit:.1f}s, current best={best_score:.9f}")
            cand, sc = _opt_full(given_a, now + limit, rng, focus_start=5.0)
            _consider(cand, sc, "given_full")

    # Phase 2: anti-palindromic optimizer on best even-length sequence.
    if best_a.size % 2 == 0:
        now = time.monotonic()
        limit = min(budget * 0.14, max(0.0, end - 45.0 - now))
        if limit > 20.0:
            anti_a = _anti_project_a(best_a)
            if anti_a is not None:
                x0 = _x_from_anti_a(anti_a)
                print(f"phase anti from best, limit={limit:.1f}s, n={anti_a.size}")
                cand, sc = _opt_anti(x0, now + limit, rng, focus_start=10.0)
                _consider(cand, sc, "anti_from_best")

    # Phase 3: discrete sign refinement of current anti-palindromic best.
    sign_x = None
    if best_a.size % 2 == 0:
        sign_x = _x_from_anti_a(best_a)
    elif given_a is not None and given_a.size % 2 == 0:
        sign_x = _x_from_anti_a(given_a)

    if sign_x is not None:
        now = time.monotonic()
        limit = min(budget * 0.06, max(0.0, end - 40.0 - now))
        if limit > 10.0:
            print(f"phase sign refine, limit={limit:.1f}s, n={2*sign_x.size}")
            cand, sc = _sign_refine(sign_x, now + limit, rng)
            if _consider(cand, sc, "sign_refine"):
                sign_x = _x_from_anti_a(best_a)

        # Refine the sign-refined support continuously.
        now = time.monotonic()
        limit = min(budget * 0.10, max(0.0, end - 40.0 - now))
        if limit > 15.0 and sign_x is not None:
            print(f"phase anti after sign, limit={limit:.1f}s, n={2*sign_x.size}")
            cand, sc = _opt_anti(sign_x, now + limit, rng, focus_start=20.0)
            _consider(cand, sc, "anti_after_sign")

    # Phase 4: Paley-character full seed (not necessarily anti-palindromic).
    now = time.monotonic()
    limit = min(budget * 0.12, max(0.0, end - 45.0 - now))
    if limit > 20.0:
        try:
            paley_a, paley_sc = _paley_best_seed(8191)
            print(f"Paley 8191 initial score={paley_sc:.9f}; optimizing limit={limit:.1f}s")
            cand, sc = _opt_full(paley_a, now + limit, rng, focus_start=8.0)
            _consider(cand, sc, "paley_8191")
        except Exception as exc:
            print("Paley seed failed:", repr(exc))

    # Phase 5: fresh large anti-palindromic start.
    now = time.monotonic()
    limit = min(budget * 0.11, max(0.0, end - 45.0 - now))
    if limit > 20.0:
        n_big = 8192
        x_big = _anti_x_greedy(n_big, rng, order="center")
        # Mix greedy and smooth to avoid pure binary traps.
        x_big = 0.65 * x_big + 0.35 * _anti_x_smooth(n_big // 2, rng, amp=2.5)
        x_big = np.clip(x_big, 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)
        print(f"phase fresh anti 8192, limit={limit:.1f}s")
        cand, sc = _opt_anti(x_big, now + limit, rng, focus_start=12.0)
        _consider(cand, sc, "fresh_anti_8192")

    # Phase 6: fresh large full start.
    now = time.monotonic()
    limit = min(budget * 0.09, max(0.0, end - 45.0 - now))
    if limit > 20.0:
        init = _random_full(8192, rng, anti=True, smooth=True, amp=3.0)
        print(f"phase fresh full 8192, limit={limit:.1f}s")
        cand, sc = _opt_full(init, now + limit, rng, focus_start=8.0)
        _consider(cand, sc, "fresh_full_8192")

    # Phase 7: second diversity anti run with a different greedy order.
    now = time.monotonic()
    limit = min(budget * 0.08, max(0.0, end - 45.0 - now))
    if limit > 20.0:
        n_div = best_a.size if (best_a.size % 2 == 0 and best_a.size >= 4096) else 8192
        x_div = _anti_x_greedy(n_div, rng, order="random")
        x_div = 0.7 * x_div + 0.3 * _anti_x_smooth(n_div // 2, rng, amp=4.0, sign=True)
        x_div = np.clip(x_div, 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)
        print(f"phase diversity anti n={n_div}, limit={limit:.1f}s")
        cand, sc = _opt_anti(x_div, now + limit, rng, focus_start=20.0)
        _consider(cand, sc, "diversity_anti")

    # Phase 8: final refinement on the best found object.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = max(0.0, end - 35.0 - now)
    if remaining > 40.0:
        if best_a.size % 2 == 0 and remaining > 80.0:
            anti_leg_limit = min(remaining * 0.45, remaining - 25.0)
            if anti_leg_limit > 20.0:
                ax = _anti_project_a(best_a)
                if ax is not None:
                    x0 = _x_from_anti_a(ax)
                    print(f"final anti refine, limit={anti_leg_limit:.1f}s, n={ax.size}")
                    cand, sc = _opt_anti(x0, now + anti_leg_limit, rng, focus_start=30.0)
                    _consider(cand, sc, "final_anti")

        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = max(0.0, end - 30.0 - now)
        if remaining > 30.0:
            print(f"final full refine, limit={remaining - 5.0:.1f}s, n={best_a.size}")
            cand, sc = _opt_full(best_a, now + remaining - 5.0, rng, focus_start=25.0)
            _consider(cand, sc, "final_full")

    # Final cheap transformations and validation.
    variant_list = []
    variant_list.append(best_a)
    if given_a is not None:
        variant_list.append(given_a)

    for var in variant_list:
        var = _clean_seq(var)
        av = _anti_project_a(var)
        if av is not None:
            variant_list.append(av)
        tv = _threshold_anti_a(var)
        if tv is not None:
            variant_list.append(tv)
        if var.size >= 32:
            nvar = var.size
            uniform = np.ones(nvar, dtype=np.float64) / float(nvar)
            for mix in (0.80, 0.90):
                variant_list.append(_clean_seq(mix * var + (1.0 - mix) * uniform))

    for k, var in enumerate(variant_list):
        try:
            var = _clean_seq(var)
            vb = _fft_square(var)
            vsc, _ = _score_with_b(var, vb)
            if np.isfinite(vsc) and vsc < best_score - 1e-14:
                best_score = vsc
                best_a = var
                print(f"variant {k} improved internal score to {best_score:.9f}, n={var.size}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_a = _clean_seq(best_a)
    best_final_score = float("inf")
    try:
        bb = _fft_square(best_a)
        best_final_score, _ = _score_with_b(best_a, bb)
    except Exception:
        best_final_score = float("inf")

    # Exact validation for reasonably sized sequences.
    best_exact = float("inf")
    final_seq = best_a

    if given_a is not None and given_a.size <= 12000:
        if not np.isfinite(given_exact) and (end - time.monotonic()) > 5.0:
            given_exact = _safe_exact(given_a)
    if best_a.size <= 12000 and (end - time.monotonic()) > 5.0:
        best_exact = _safe_exact(best_a)

    if np.isfinite(best_exact) and np.isfinite(given_exact):
        if best_exact <= given_exact + 1e-12:
            final_seq = best_a
        else:
            final_seq = given_a
    elif np.isfinite(best_exact):
        final_seq = best_a
    elif np.isfinite(given_exact):
        # If the optimized candidate could not be exactly validated, prefer the
        # known exact candidate unless the internal gain is very large.
        if best_final_score < given_exact - 1e-3:
            final_seq = best_a
        else:
            final_seq = given_a
    else:
        final_seq = best_a

    final_seq = _clean_seq(final_seq)
    fb = _fft_square(final_seq)
    final_internal, _ = _score_with_b(final_seq, fb)

    try:
        exact = _safe_exact(final_seq)
        print(f"final internal={final_internal:.9f}, exact={exact:.9f}, n={final_seq.size}")
    except Exception:
        print(f"final internal={final_internal:.9f}, n={final_seq.size}")

    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```
